PWC_ai_war_targets_U00_balanced = {
	allowed = { tag = U00 }
	enable = {
		NOT = { pwc_st_protection_active = yes }
		pwc_st_ai_war_pacing_ready = yes
		is_ai = yes
		has_country_flag = pwc_ai_balanced
	}
	abort_when_not_enabled = yes
	ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = U01 value = 100 }
	ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = U02 value = 100 }
}

	enable = {
		NOT = { pwc_st_protection_active = yes }
		pwc_st_ai_war_pacing_ready = yes
		is_ai = yes
		has_country_flag = pwc_ai_defense
	}
	abort_when_not_enabled = yes
	ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = V98 value = 90 }
	ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = W13 value = 90 }
	ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = W17 value = 90 }

	ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = W44 value = 90 }
